--- Commit Message ---
LU-19276 tests: Verify fs/pool/lqa/nodemap names

llapi_name_validate() was introduced as a common helper to check
whether a name is valid. Add tests to verify various filesystem,
OST pool, LQA or nodemap names.

Also rename sanity-sec test_8 to test_8a and add a version check
for the invalid lqa names in sanity-quota test_97a.

Test-Parameters: trivial testlist=conf-sanity env=ONLY=60c
Test-Parameters: testlist=ost-pools env=ONLY=1l,8
Test-Parameters: testlist=sanity-quota env=ONLY=27e,27f,27g,97a
Test-Parameters: testlist=sanity-sec env=ONLY=8a,8b
Test-Parameters: serverversion=2.17 testlist=conf-sanity env=ONLY=60c
Test-Parameters: serverversion=2.17 testlist=ost-pools env=ONLY=1l,8
Test-Parameters: serverversion=2.17 testlist=sanity-quota env=ONLY=27e,27f,27g,97a
Test-Parameters: serverversion=2.17 testlist=sanity-sec env=ONLY=8a,8b
